### greet-zh


### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？

```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner tu rama al día con la base o limpiar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, siempre que nadie más trabaje sobre esa rama. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar el historial tal cual ocurrió.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接的，保证数据按序、不丢不重地送达；UDP 是无连接、不保证可靠的，发了就不管，换来的是更低的延迟和开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want a clean, linear history on your own branch before sharing it, like tidying up local commits before a pull request. Merge when the branch is already shared or you want to keep the true history of when work was joined.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change is written down before it is applied to the database itself. If the system crashes midway, the database can replay that log to recover exactly what was committed and nothing half-finished.
```

```
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五北京到上海 · 600 元以下</div>

<div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没有数据，每天早上会更新</div>
<script>
function render(rows) {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!rows || !rows.length) { empty.hidden = false; return; }
  empty.hidden = true;
  var under = 0;
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = r.train;
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = r.seats || '';
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = '¥' + r.fare + (r.fare < 600 ? '' : '（超过 600）');
    if (r.fare < 600) { under++; } else { row.style.opacity = '0.5'; }
    row.appendChild(main); row.appendChild(sub); row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  document.getElementById('n').textContent = under + ' 班';
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = '600 元以下的班次，每天早上更新';
}
var v = ducter.get('fares');
Promise.resolve(v).then(render);
ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get('fares')).then(render); });
</script>
```

```
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">Not run yet</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Writes per second and p99 latency</div>

<script>
function render() {
  var d = ducter.get('runs') || {runs: []};
  var runs = d.runs || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  if (!runs.length) { list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">The first run will show here.</div>'; return; }
  var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('latest').textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls: ' + (last.stalls == null ? '–' : last.stalls);
  list.innerHTML = runs.slice().reverse().map(function (r) {
    return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + r.night + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' · stalls ' + (r.stalls == null ? '–' : r.stalls) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">' + r.writes + '/s</div></div>';
  }).join('');
  ducter.summary(last.night + ': ' + last.writes + ' writes/s, p99 ' + last.p99);
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>
```

```
    ducter.set('items', items);
    resetForm();
    render();
  };

  $('days').onclick = function (e) {
    var row = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.k-row') : null;
    if (!row) return;
    var i = Number(row.getAttribute('data-i'));
    var x = items[i];
    if (!x) return;
    editIndex = i;
    $('day').value = x.day;
    $('what').value = x.what;
    $('amt').value = x.amount;
    $('hint').textContent = '正在改这一笔';
    $('save').textContent = '保存修改';
    $('cancel').style.display = '';
    $('del').style.display = '';
    $('addbox').open = true;
  };

  // Keep the day field from the last entry for quick repeat entries.
  $('save').addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (editIndex < 0 && items.length) $('day').value = items[items.length - 1].day;
  });

  ducter.onChange(function () { load(); render(); });
  load();
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
```

```
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const total = xs.reduce((s, x) => s + (Number(x.yuan) || 0), 0);
  const totalEl = document.getElementById("total");
  const subEl = document.getElementById("sub");
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  if (xs.length) {
    totalEl.textContent = "¥" + total.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    subEl.textContent = xs.length + " 笔";
  } else {
    totalEl.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
    subEl.textContent = "还没有花销";
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔,合计 ¥" + total.toLocaleString("zh-CN") : "还没记");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  if (!xs.length) {
    const e = document.createElement("div");
    e.className = "k-empty";
    e.textContent = "记下的花销会列在这里";
    list.appendChild(e);
    return;
  }
  for (const x of xs) {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + Number(x.yuan).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}
```

```
  }

  document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
```

```
<div class="k-sum">

  <span class="k-sum-value k-none" id="total">还没记</span>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">记下第一笔，合计会在这里</div>
</div>

<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>暂无花销</div>
</div>

<details class="k-add">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-field"><label for="trip">名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" placeholder="比如 高铁 G1"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="day">日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><div class="k-chips" id="cats"></div></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="amt">金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
  </div>
</details>
</main>
<script>
var cats = ['交通', '住宿', '餐饮', '其他'];
var cat = '交通';
function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
function esc(s) {
  return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
    return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]; … = money(total);
    t.classList.remove('k-none');
    $('sub').textContent = xs.length + ' 笔';
  } else {
    t.textContent = '还没记';
    t.classList.add('k-none');
    $('sub').textContent = '记下第一笔，合计会在这里';
  }
  $('empty').hidden = xs.length > 0;
  $('list').innerHTML = xs.map(function (x) {
    return '<div class="k-row">'
      + '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(x.trip || '未命名')
      + '<div class="k-row-sub"><span>' + esc(x.cat || '其他') + '</span>'
      + (x.day ? '<span>' + esc(x.day) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></div>'
      + '<div class="k-row-value">' + money(x.amt) + '</div></div>';
  }).join('');
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length + ' 笔,合计 ' + money(total) : '还没记');
}
$('cats').onclick = function (e) {
  var b = e.target.closest('.k-chip');
  if (!b) return;
  cat = b.getAttribute('data-c');
  renderChips();
};
$('save').onclick = function () {
  var amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if (!amt) return;
  var xs = ducter.get('items') || [];
  xs.push({
    trip: $('trip').value.trim(),
    day: $('day').value.trim(),
    cat: cat,
    amt: amt
  });
  ducter.set('items', xs);
  $('amt').value = '';
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
renderChips();
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}
```

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让它在那个 TTL 测试的分支上再补一条，覆盖刚好到期那一刻。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

在现有的 TTL 假时钟测试基础上，再补一条测试，覆盖时间恰好等于过期时刻的情况：
- 到期时刻（时钟正好走到 TTL 边界）时，值应视为已过期、取不到。
- 到期前一刻（比边界少 1 毫秒或最小时间单位）时，值仍然有效。
- 只用假时钟推进时间，不用真实 sleep 或真实计时。

完成标准：在同一分支上提交，跑完整测试套件并报告结果，并连续跑 20 次确认不闪烁。范围外：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。
  [retitled task ttl] foo: TTL 测试不稳定，并补到期时刻的测试
```
